<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-48309233" data-id="48309233" data-tags="Education" data-score="240" data-comments="270" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48309233" data-time="1779977580">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.latimes.com/california/story/2026-05-27/uc-math-professors-demand-return-of-sat-for-stem-admissions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/6a6f7ae/2147483647/strip/true/crop/5472x2873+0+388/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F71%2F9a%2Fa7e16b5640b59762cc3e758ffc1a%2F1294188-me-uc-irvine-student-housing-001-ajs.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='latimes.com' data-c='#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.latimes.com/california/story/2026-05-27/uc-math-professors-demand-return-of-sat-for-stem-admissions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Citing &#x27;severe&#x27; math deficits, UC faculty demand a return to SAT tests for STEM</span><span class='zh'>因数学能力被认为严重不足，UC 教职员要求在 STEM 招生中恢复 SAT 考试</span></a><span class="dom">latimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>UC faculty cite severe math deficits and push for reinstating SATs in STEM admissions, fueling the broader debate over standardized tests in higher education.</span><span class='zh'>UC 教职员以数学能力严重不足为由，推动 STEM 招生重新采用 SAT 等标准化考试，成为高等教育招生标准化考试辩论的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>UC faculty cite severe math deficits and push for reinstating SATs in STEM admissions, fueling the broader debate over standardized tests in higher education. Supporters argue tests help identify quantitative potential, while critics warn about equity and access implications.</span><span class='zh'>UC 教职员以数学能力严重不足为由，推动 STEM 招生重新采用 SAT 等标准化考试，成为高等教育招生标准化考试辩论的一部分。支持者认为考试能筛出具备定量潜力的候选人，反对者则担忧公平性与机会不均。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>20.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>240</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48309233' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>270</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48307887" data-id="48307887" data-tags="AI" data-score="419" data-comments="286" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48307887" data-time="1779970819">
<a class="thumb" href="https://lenz.io/research/llm-disagreement" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://lenz.io/research/llm-disagreement/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='lenz.io' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lenz.io/research/llm-disagreement" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Five frontier LLMs disagree on 67% of 1k real-world fact-check claims</span><span class='zh'>五大前沿LLM在1000条真实世界事实核查中对67%存在分歧</span></a><span class="dom">lenz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Five frontier LLMs disagree on about 67% of 1,000 real-world fact-check claims, underscoring reliability gaps in current models for factual tasks.</span><span class='zh'>在对1000条真实世界事实核查的对比评测中，五大前沿大模型在约67%的样本上意见不一致，暴露了当前模型在事实性任务上的可靠性不足。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Five frontier LLMs disagree on about 67% of 1,000 real-world fact-check claims, underscoring reliability gaps in current models for factual tasks. The results emphasize the need for external verification or ensemble approaches before deploying these models for journalism, search, or moderation.</span><span class='zh'>在对1000条真实世界事实核查的对比评测中，五大前沿大模型在约67%的样本上意见不一致，暴露了当前模型在事实性任务上的可靠性不足。结果强调需要额外的验证或集成策略，方能在新闻、搜索或内容审核等场景中安全使用。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://itsfoss.com/news/amd-vivado-bait-and-switch-on-linux-users/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AMD pulls a bait-and-switch on Linux users with Vivado licensing changes</span><span class='zh'>AMD 对 Linux 用户的 Vivado 许可变更引发争议</span></a><span class="dom">itsfoss.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AMD&#x27;s Vivado licensing changes reportedly shift terms for Linux users, raising concerns about compatibility, pricing, and the openness of the FPGA ecosystem after AMD&#x27;s Xilinx acquisition.</span><span class='zh'>Vivado 许可变更被指向 Linux 用户设定更严格或更高成本的条款，可能影响 FPGA 开发者的工作流，尤其中在 AMD 收购 Xilinx 后。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AMD&#x27;s Vivado licensing changes reportedly shift terms for Linux users, raising concerns about compatibility, pricing, and the openness of the FPGA ecosystem after AMD&#x27;s Xilinx acquisition. The move could disrupt workflows for teams relying on Xilinx IP, pushing developers toward tighter licenses or alternative tools.</span><span class='zh'>Vivado 许可变更被指向 Linux 用户设定更严格或更高成本的条款，可能影响 FPGA 开发者的工作流，尤其中在 AMD 收购 Xilinx 后。此举可能促使开发者寻求替代工具或更紧密的授权控制。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mlsu.io/posts/day-off/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can we have the day off?</span><span class='zh'>我们能放假吗？</span></a><span class="dom">mlsu.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflection arguing for institutional acceptance of taking full days off as a normal productivity and wellbeing tool rather than a luxury or punishment.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张把整天休息视为常规的生产力与幸福工具，而非特权或惩罚。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflection arguing for institutional acceptance of taking full days off as a normal productivity and wellbeing tool rather than a luxury or punishment. It emphasizes that deliberate rest, clear boundaries and team norms around time off improve long-term output and morale.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张把整天休息视为常规的生产力与幸福工具，而非特权或惩罚。强调有意识的休息、明确的界限和团队对请假的共识，能提升长期产出与士气。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.youtube/news-and-events/improving-ai-labels-viewers-creators/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://variety.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/YouTube.png?crop=0px%2C8px%2C1024px%2C576px&amp;resize=1000%2C563' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.youtube' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.youtube/news-and-events/improving-ai-labels-viewers-creators/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>YouTube to automatically label AI-generated videos</span><span class='zh'>YouTube 将自动标注 AI 生成视频</span></a><span class="dom">blog.youtube</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>YouTube is rolling out automated labeling for AI-generated videos to help viewers identify synthetic content.</span><span class='zh'>YouTube 正在推出自动标注 AI 生成视频的功能，以帮助观众辨识合成内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>YouTube is rolling out automated labeling for AI-generated videos to help viewers identify synthetic content. The system aims to increase transparency and reduce misinformation, with tags appearing in search, recommendations, and thumbnails. The move will affect creators who leverage AI and could gradually normalize disclosure signals across the platform.</span><span class='zh'>YouTube 正在推出自动标注 AI 生成视频的功能，以帮助观众辨识合成内容。该机制旨在提升透明度并降低误导风险，标签可能出现在搜索与推荐等位面。此举将影响使用 AI 的创作者，并推动在平台上逐步规范披露信号。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://orchidfiles.com/im-tired-of-ai-generated-answers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;m Tired of Talking to AI</span><span class='zh'>我厌倦和 AI 交谈</span></a><span class="dom">orchidfiles.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece captures fatigue with AI-generated answers and a longing for more trustworthy, human-like interaction.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章表达了对 AI 生成回答的疲惫，渴望更可信、可核验的互动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece captures fatigue with AI-generated answers and a longing for more trustworthy, human-like interaction. It calls for clearer sources, transparent reasoning, and design choices that favor meaningful dialogue over generic polish.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章表达了对 AI 生成回答的疲惫，渴望更可信、可核验的互动。作者呼吁提供来源、提高推理透明度，并在设计上优先实现有意义的对话，而非泛泛的回答。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.axios.com/2026/05/28/ai-spending-roi-enterprise-costs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI sticker shock hits corporate America</span><span class='zh'>AI 价格冲击波及美国企业</span></a><span class="dom">axios.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI sticker shock hits corporate America as tool costs, data infrastructure, and governance needs outpace expectations for ROI.</span><span class='zh'>AI 价格冲击波及美国企业，工具成本、数据基础设施和治理要求提升，超出初期预期。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI sticker shock hits corporate America as tool costs, data infrastructure, and governance needs outpace expectations for ROI. Firms face budgeting challenges, vendor lock-in risk, and a push to strengthen MLOps to scale responsibly.</span><span class='zh'>AI 价格冲击波及美国企业，工具成本、数据基础设施和治理要求提升，超出初期预期。公司需要权衡成本、供应商锁定风险，并强化 MLOps 来实现可持续规模化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/May/27/product-market-fit/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I think Anthropic and OpenAI have found product-market fit</span><span class='zh'>我认为 Anthropic 与 OpenAI 已找到产品-市场契合点</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that Anthropic and OpenAI have achieved product-market fit, suggesting their APIs and tooling are resonating with developers and enterprises.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 Anthropic 与 OpenAI 已找到产品-市场契合点，表明它们的 API 与工具已经得到开发者和企业的共鸣。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that Anthropic and OpenAI have achieved product-market fit, suggesting their APIs and tooling are resonating with developers and enterprises. The view reflects a maturing AI ecosystem where practical, scalable offerings begin to outpace novelty. It highlights what successful AI tooling needs to scale.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 Anthropic 与 OpenAI 已找到产品-市场契合点，表明它们的 API 与工具已经得到开发者和企业的共鸣。这一观点反映出 AI 生态系统逐步成熟，实用、可扩展的产品开始超越新奇性。也点出成功的 AI 工具要能实现规模化的关键要素。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pcgamer.com/hardware/duckduckgos-ai-free-search-saw-nearly-28-percent-more-visits-in-the-week-following-googles-insistence-that-people-love-ai-mode/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DuckDuckGo search saw 28% more visits after Google said people love AI mode</span><span class='zh'>DuckDuckGo 搜索在 Google 宣称人们喜爱 AI 模式后访量上涨 28%</span></a><span class="dom">pcgamer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DuckDuckGo saw roughly 28% more visits in the week after Google publicly touted AI mode, signaling interest in AI-enabled search options beyond the big players.</span><span class='zh'>在 Google 公布 AI 模式受欢迎的一周内，DuckDuckGo 的访问量增加了约 28%，显示 AI 功能对隐私导向用户的吸引力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DuckDuckGo saw roughly 28% more visits in the week after Google publicly touted AI mode, signaling interest in AI-enabled search options beyond the big players. The shift hints at how privacy-focused users respond to mainstream AI features and where alternative search engines fit in.</span><span class='zh'>在 Google 公布 AI 模式受欢迎的一周内，DuckDuckGo 的访问量增加了约 28%，显示 AI 功能对隐私导向用户的吸引力。此现象揭示隐私取向的用户在主流 AI 功能出现时如何寻找替代方案，以及替代搜索引擎的定位。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hallucinate.site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hallucinate – Massively Multiplayer Online Rave</span><span class='zh'>幻觉：大型多人在线电子派对</span></a><span class="dom">hallucinate.site</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A browser-based virtual rave that mixes real-time audiovisuals, avatars and persistent shared spaces to let many participants party together.</span><span class='zh'>一个基于浏览器的虚拟派对平台，将实时视听、头像和持久共享空间组合成多人在线体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A browser-based virtual rave that mixes real-time audiovisuals, avatars and persistent shared spaces to let many participants party together. It treats a rave as an MMO—prioritizing emergent social interactions, live visuals and music synchronization rather than a tightly structured event.</span><span class='zh'>一个基于浏览器的虚拟派对平台，将实时视听、头像和持久共享空间组合成多人在线体验。它把电子派对当作 MMO 来做，强调自发的社交互动、现场视觉与音乐同步，而不是单一的结构化活动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>327</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48304260' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>145</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48302151" data-id="48302151" data-tags="Security" data-score="427" data-comments="344" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48302151" data-time="1779924209">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/05/27/us/politics/fbi-arrest-cia-official-gold-bars.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FBI Arrests CIA Official with $40M in Gold Bars in His Home</span><span class='zh'>FBI逮捕中情局官员，家中搜出价值 4,000 万美元的金条</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A national-security employee was arrested after authorities found about $40 million in gold bars at his residence, triggering criminal charges and scrutiny over potential financial misconduct.</span><span class='zh'>一名情报人员因住所被查获约价值 4,000 万美元的金条而遭逮捕，并面临刑事指控，事件引发对其财务行为的审查。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A national-security employee was arrested after authorities found about $40 million in gold bars at his residence, triggering criminal charges and scrutiny over potential financial misconduct. The case raises questions about how intelligence personnel are monitored for unexplained wealth and the intersection of internal security and federal law enforcement.</span><span class='zh'>一名情报人员因住所被查获约价值 4,000 万美元的金条而遭逮捕，并面临刑事指控，事件引发对其财务行为的审查。该案凸显了对情报人员无合理解释财富的监管缺口，以及内部安全与联邦执法之间的交集。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://support.last.fm/t/last-fm-is-now-independent/118591" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Last.fm is now independent</span><span class='zh'>Last.fm 现在独立运营</span></a><span class="dom">support.last.fm</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Last.fm has achieved independence from its former owner, enabling autonomous product decisions and potentially different privacy posture.</span><span class='zh'>Last.fm 已实现独立运营，能自行决策产品路线并可能调整隐私策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Last.fm has achieved independence from its former owner, enabling autonomous product decisions and potentially different privacy posture. The move matters for long-time users and the sustainability of the platform in a competitive music landscape. It signals a wish to recalibrate how the service fits into modern open ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Last.fm 已实现独立运营，能自行决策产品路线并可能调整隐私策略。在竞争激烈的音乐生态中，这一举措关系到长期用户的体验与平台的可持续性。也显示出在现代开源生态中重新定位服务定位的愿望。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/27/tech-ceos-are-apparently-suffering-from-ai-psychosis/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tech CEOs are apparently suffering from AI psychosis</span><span class='zh'>科技CEO 似乎在 AI 热潮中陷入‘AI 精神错乱’</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tech CEOs are reportedly suffering from AI psychosis, a tongue-in-cheek description of how leaders react to AI hype.</span><span class='zh'>科技界 CEO 被称在 AI 热潮中经历“AI 精神错乱”，用以描述领导者对 AI 热度的快速反应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tech CEOs are reportedly suffering from AI psychosis, a tongue-in-cheek description of how leaders react to AI hype. The piece argues that rapid shifts in AI narratives are reshaping strategy, hiring, and governance, sometimes more than actual product outcomes. This dynamic matters for investors and engineers plotting long-term roadmaps.</span><span class='zh'>科技界 CEO 被称在 AI 热潮中经历“AI 精神错乱”，用以描述领导者对 AI 热度的快速反应。文章认为 AI 叙事的迅速变化正在重塑战略、招聘与治理，有时影响力甚至超过实际产品成果。这一动态对投资者和工程师制定长期路线图至关重要。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/may/27/canada-sweden-saab-globaleye-aircraft" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Canada to order military plane fleet from Sweden in shift from US suppliers</span><span class='zh'>加拿大将从瑞典订购军机机队，转向非美供应商</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Canada plans to order a Swedish-built military aircraft fleet, diversifying away from traditional US suppliers.</span><span class='zh'>加拿大计划从瑞典采购军事飞行器机队，寻求摆脱对美国供应商的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Canada plans to order a Swedish-built military aircraft fleet, diversifying away from traditional US suppliers. The move signals a strategic shift in defense procurement and alliance dynamics, with implications for industrial partnerships, maintenance, and interoperability. The contract may reshape North American defense supply chains.</span><span class='zh'>加拿大计划从瑞典采购军事飞行器机队，寻求摆脱对美国供应商的依赖。这一举措体现了防务采购的战略调整及同盟关系的变化，对产业伙伴关系、维护与互操作性带来影响，可能重塑北美防务供应链。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iisc.ac.in/a-eureka-machine-that-thinks-like-nature-and-explores-what-ai-cannot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Eureka machine that thinks like nature and explores what AI cannot</span><span class='zh'>像自然一样思考的尤里卡机器：探索AI无法胜任的领域</span></a><span class="dom">iisc.ac.in</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A nature-inspired &#x27;Eureka&#x27; machine tackles problems that standard AI struggles with, hinting at new computational paradigms.</span><span class='zh'>一台受自然启发的“尤里卡”机器，尝试解决传统AI难以胜任的问题，揭示了新的计算范式潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A nature-inspired &#x27;Eureka&#x27; machine tackles problems that standard AI struggles with, hinting at new computational paradigms. By borrowing strategies from nature—adaptation, robustness, and self-organization—it reveals limits of current AI and points toward future research directions.</span><span class='zh'>一台受自然启发的“尤里卡”机器，尝试解决传统AI难以胜任的问题，揭示了新的计算范式潜力。通过借鉴自然界的适应性、鲁棒性与自我组织等策略，展示了现有AI的局限并指向未来的研究方向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnbc.com/2026/05/27/google-employee-polymarket-insider-trading.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google employee charged with $1M Polymarket insider trading bet on search term</span><span class='zh'>谷歌员工因在 Polymarket 上用内线信息下注 100 万美元被起诉</span></a><span class="dom">cnbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An employee faces charges for allegedly using privileged access to place a roughly $1M bet on a prediction market tied to a search-term outcome, invoking insider trading laws in a novel context.</span><span class='zh'>一名员工被控利用特权访问在一个预测市场上下注约 100 万美元，下注标的是与搜索词相关的结果，这一指控把传统内线交易法规带入了新型市场。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An employee faces charges for allegedly using privileged access to place a roughly $1M bet on a prediction market tied to a search-term outcome, invoking insider trading laws in a novel context. The case highlights how traditional securities rules and surveillance can apply to decentralized or nontraditional markets when insider knowledge is used for profit.</span><span class='zh'>一名员工被控利用特权访问在一个预测市场上下注约 100 万美元，下注标的是与搜索词相关的结果，这一指控把传统内线交易法规带入了新型市场。此案强调当使用内部信息牟利时，传统的监管与监控手段也会适用于去中心化或非传统的交易平台。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thran.uk/writ/hdid/2025/12/simcity-3k-in-4k.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SimCity 3k in 4k (2025)</span><span class='zh'>SimCity 3000 4K 重制（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">thran.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A fan-made project recreates SimCity 3000 in 4K, updating visuals while preserving the classic city-building gameplay.</span><span class='zh'>一个玩家自制项目将 SimCity 3000 重制为 4K，保留经典玩法的同时提升画质。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A fan-made project recreates SimCity 3000 in 4K, updating visuals while preserving the classic city-building gameplay. The effort highlights how modern upscaling, art direction, and emulation can breathe new life into beloved 2000s games. For retro enthusiasts and streamers, it signals a growing niche of high-res nostalgia experiences.</span><span class='zh'>一个玩家自制项目将 SimCity 3000 重制为 4K，保留经典玩法的同时提升画质。此举展示了现代放大、美术风格更新与仿真技术如何为经典游戏注入新活力。对于怀旧玩家和主播而言，预示着高分辨率的复古体验日益兴起。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.jacquescorbytuech.com/writing/what-apple-and-google-are-doing-your-push-notifications" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What Apple and Google are doing to push notifications</span><span class='zh'>苹果与谷歌正在如何改造你的推送通知</span></a><span class="dom">jacquescorbytuech.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple and Google are tightening push notification controls with new privacy safeguards and user-centric options.</span><span class='zh'>苹果与谷歌在推送通知方面加强了隐私保护和用户导向的控制，推出更清晰的授权、分级通知和更智能的投递策略以减少骚扰。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple and Google are tightening push notification controls with new privacy safeguards and user-centric options. The changes include clearer opt-ins, quieter notification channels, and smarter delivery to reduce spam while preserving essential alerts. Developers will need to adapt to new policies and API limitations.</span><span class='zh'>苹果与谷歌在推送通知方面加强了隐私保护和用户导向的控制，推出更清晰的授权、分级通知和更智能的投递策略以减少骚扰。开发者将需要调整应用以适应新的政策和 API 限制，影响通知体验与广告生态。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rubbishtalk.com/economy/how-private-equity-bought-americas-essential-services/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Private equity bought America&#x27;s essential services</span><span class='zh'>私募股权买下美国的基础公共服务</span></a><span class="dom">rubbishtalk.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that private equity has increasingly acquired essential public-facing services, raising concerns about debt loads, cost-cutting, and long-term resilience.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出私募股权日益收购关键公共服务，带来债务压力、成本削减和长期韧性方面的担忧，可能影响可负担性与可及性，同时揭示资本密集型行业中的激励机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that private equity has increasingly acquired essential public-facing services, raising concerns about debt loads, cost-cutting, and long-term resilience. It highlights potential risks to affordability and access while outlining the incentives at play in capital-heavy sectors.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出私募股权日益收购关键公共服务，带来债务压力、成本削减和长期韧性方面的担忧，可能影响可负担性与可及性，同时揭示资本密集型行业中的激励机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/games/938340/valve-steam-deck-price-increase" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Valve raises Steam Deck prices</span><span class='zh'>Valve 上调 Steam Deck 价格</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Valve raises Steam Deck prices, potentially narrowing the device&#x27;s edge against handheld rivals amid ongoing supply chain pressures.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 提升 Steam Deck 价格，反映了硬件成本压力及市场定价策略对掌机竞争力的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Valve raises Steam Deck prices, potentially narrowing the device&#x27;s edge against handheld rivals amid ongoing supply chain pressures. The price hike could slow adoption among budget-conscious gamers and affect the broader handheld PC market.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 提升 Steam Deck 价格，反映了硬件成本压力及市场定价策略对掌机竞争力的影响。价格上调可能拖慢对预算敏感玩家的吸引力，并对掌机 PC 市场格局产生连锁效应。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://sverre.me/blog/rust-on-kindle/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sverre.me/processed_images/kindle_slint.4fe7d110ed3ed9bf.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sverre.me' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sverre.me/blog/rust-on-kindle/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rust (and Slint) on a Jailbroken Kindle</span><span class='zh'>在越狱 Kindle 上运行 Rust 与 Slint</span></a><span class="dom">sverre.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A maker demonstrates running Rust and the Slint UI toolkit on a jailbroken Kindle, showing that modern language ecosystems can reach constrained devices.</span><span class='zh'>一位爱好者在越狱的 Kindle 上演示了 Rust 及 Slint 用户界面工具包的运行，证明紧凑的运行时和跨编译能够把现代语言生态带到受限设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A maker demonstrates running Rust and the Slint UI toolkit on a jailbroken Kindle, showing that modern language ecosystems can reach constrained devices. The experiment highlights how compact runtimes and cross-compilation enable interactive interfaces on e-readers.</span><span class='zh'>一位爱好者在越狱的 Kindle 上演示了 Rust 及 Slint 用户界面工具包的运行，证明紧凑的运行时和跨编译能够把现代语言生态带到受限设备。此类尝试凸显了在低功耗电子书阅读器上实现交互式界面的可行性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22391" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All of human cooking compressed into 2 megabytes</span><span class='zh'>把人类烹饪知识压缩到 2MB</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study presents a compact representation of cooking knowledge compressed into about 2 megabytes, enabling recipe generation with minimal data.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究提出把烹饪知识压缩至约 2MB 的紧凑表征，仍能生成多样化的食谱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study presents a compact representation of cooking knowledge compressed into about 2 megabytes, enabling recipe generation with minimal data. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The narrator recounts a disastrously misaligned interview that left them frustrated and questioning the company’s process.</span><span class='zh'>叙述者讲述了一次极为失配的面试经历，过程让人感到沮丧并对公司的招聘流程产生质疑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The narrator recounts a disastrously misaligned interview that left them frustrated and questioning the company’s process. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://medium.com/@jakeorlowitz/wikipedia-is-doing-the-capitalist-thing-56a393232943" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Big tech&#x27;s anti-labor playbook has come for Wikipedia</span><span class='zh'>大型科技公司的反劳工手法也延伸至维基百科</span></a><span class="dom">medium.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critical piece argues that the anti-labor playbook favored by big tech has infiltrated Wikipedia, shaping editor dynamics, governance, and content moderation.</span><span class='zh'>文章批评大型科技公司用来压制劳工的手法已渗透到维基百科，影响编辑社区的治理与内容管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critical piece argues that the anti-labor playbook favored by big tech has infiltrated Wikipedia, shaping editor dynamics, governance, and content moderation. The piece warns that corporate pressure and labor suppression tactics threaten Wikipedia’s open, collaborative model and could impact information quality and editor rights.</span><span class='zh'>文章批评大型科技公司用来压制劳工的手法已渗透到维基百科，影响编辑社区的治理与内容管理。警示称企业压力与压制劳动的策略可能侵蚀维基百科的开放协作模式，进而影响信息质量与编辑者权益。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://posthog.com/blog/training-ai-models" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Training our own AI models</span><span class='zh'>训练我们自己的 AI 模型</span></a><span class="dom">posthog.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PostHog argues for training our own AI models, outlining advantages like data control, privacy, and customization plus the risks of vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>PostHog 讨论在本地或自有环境训练自家 AI 模型的可行性，强调数据控制、隐私保护、定制能力与避免供应商锁定等优点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PostHog argues for training our own AI models, outlining advantages like data control, privacy, and customization plus the risks of vendor lock-in. It maps a practical path from data collection to model selection, evaluation, and governance, along with cost considerations.</span><span class='zh'>PostHog 讨论在本地或自有环境训练自家 AI 模型的可行性，强调数据控制、隐私保护、定制能力与避免供应商锁定等优点。文中给出从数据准备、模型选型到评估与治理的实用路径，并分析成本与取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/77273" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go: Support for Generic Methods</span><span class='zh'>Go：支持泛型方法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go adds support for generics and generic methods, enabling more reusable code and type-safe abstractions.</span><span class='zh'>Go 新增对泛型及泛型方法的支持，提升代码重用性与类型安全的抽象能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go adds support for generics and generic methods, enabling more reusable code and type-safe abstractions. This marks a major evolution for the language, improving ergonomics for library authors and API designers. Expect broader adoption in real-world projects as patterns become clearer.</span><span class='zh'>Go 新增对泛型及泛型方法的支持，提升代码重用性与类型安全的抽象能力。这是语言发展中的一次重要演进，将提升库作者与 API 设计者的工作效率，随着使用模式逐步明朗，预计在实际项目中的应用将更广泛。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New study shows that stress disrupts the hippocampus integrates overlapping events, impairing memory inference. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stripe has earned a reputation for being forgiving toward &#x27;friendly fraud&#x27; claims, which can let customers dispute charges even when services were delivered.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章讨论 Stripe 在所谓的“友好欺诈”情形上的宽容态度，以及对商家可能造成的风险与成本。作者分析了风险策略的权衡，以及对商家收入和信任生态的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://en.ilsole24ore.com/art/lombardy-introduces-increased-charges-of-up-to-200-per-cent-for-data-centre-construction-in-green-and-agricultural-areas-AI6Jp4ID" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lombardy increases charges for the construction of data centres in green areas</span><span class='zh'>伦巴第提高在绿色区域建设数据中心的收费</span></a><span class="dom">en.ilsole24ore.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lombardy increases charges for constructing data centers in green or agricultural zones by up to 200%, in an effort to curb environmental impact.</span><span class='zh'>伦巴第地区提高在绿色或农业用地建设数据中心的费用，最高可达200%，以控制环境影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lombardy increases charges for constructing data centers in green or agricultural zones by up to 200%, in an effort to curb environmental impact. The policy alters data center economics and site selection, with potential spillovers for regional investment.</span><span class='zh'>伦巴第地区提高在绿色或农业用地建设数据中心的费用，最高可达200%，以控制环境影响。此举将影响数据中心的经济性与选址决策，并可能影响区域投资。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/05/erin-brockovich-made-a-map-to-track-data-centers-around-the-country/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Erin Brockovich made a map to track data centers around the country</span><span class='zh'>艾琳·布罗克维奇绘制的数据中心分布地图</span></a><span class="dom">niemanlab.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Erin Brockovich creates a nationwide map to track data centers, highlighting energy use and environmental implications of the data economy.</span><span class='zh'>艾琳·布罗克维奇绘制了一张覆盖全美的数据中心分布地图，瞄准揭示能源消耗与数据经济的环境影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Erin Brockovich creates a nationwide map to track data centers, highlighting energy use and environmental implications of the data economy. The map serves as a tool for journalists and policymakers to scrutinize infrastructure footprints.</span><span class='zh'>艾琳·布罗克维奇绘制了一张覆盖全美的数据中心分布地图，瞄准揭示能源消耗与数据经济的环境影响。地图成为记者与政策制定者审视基础设施足迹的工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-strange-melancholy-of-slaying-monsters/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Melancholy of Slaying Monsters</span><span class='zh'>屠怪之忧郁</span></a><span class="dom">thereader.mitpress.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores the emotional texture of monster-slaying narratives and why victory can feel melancholic rather than purely triumphant.</span><span class='zh'>探索怪物屠杀叙事背后的情感纹理，阐释为何胜利有时会带来哀愁而非纯粹的胜利感。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores the emotional texture of monster-slaying narratives and why victory can feel melancholic rather than purely triumphant. It argues that the act of defeating monsters often leaves players with a sense of responsibility, fatigue, or moral ambiguity, shaping how designers tell stories and design systems.</span><span class='zh'>探索怪物屠杀叙事背后的情感纹理，阐释为何胜利有时会带来哀愁而非纯粹的胜利感。作者认为击败怪物的过程常伴随责任感、疲惫感或道德模糊，这也影响设计者如何讲述故事与构建系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pop.rdi.sh/where-does-next-token-prediction-leave-us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Where does next-token prediction leave us?</span><span class='zh'>下一次标记预测能把我们带到何处？</span></a><span class="dom">pop.rdi.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that while next-token prediction has driven impressive gains, it still lacks robust reasoning, grounding, and safe control.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为单纯的下一个标记预测已取得显著进展，但在推理、语义落地与安全控制方面仍有短板，指向对齐评估与超越纯自回归的混合方法研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that while next-token prediction has driven impressive gains, it still lacks robust reasoning, grounding, and safe control. It points toward evaluating alignment and exploring hybrid approaches beyond pure autoregression.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为单纯的下一个标记预测已取得显著进展，但在推理、语义落地与安全控制方面仍有短板，指向对齐评估与超越纯自回归的混合方法研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.seangoedecke.com/the-just-say-no-engineer-was-a-zirp-phenomenon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The just-say-no engineer was a ZIRP phenomenon</span><span class='zh'>“说不干”的工程师是 ZIRP 时期的产物</span></a><span class="dom">seangoedecke.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that the &#x27;just-say-no engineer&#x27; persona emerged under macro conditions associated with ZIRP, where funding and incentives shaped risk tolerance in engineering culture.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为“说不干”的工程师形象是在与 ZIRP 相关的宏观环境下成长起来的，资金与激励影响了工程文化中的风险偏好，并讨论了金融环境如何影响技术决策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that the &#x27;just-say-no engineer&#x27; persona emerged under macro conditions associated with ZIRP, where funding and incentives shaped risk tolerance in engineering culture. It examines how financial environments influence technical decision-making.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为“说不干”的工程师形象是在与 ZIRP 相关的宏观环境下成长起来的，资金与激励影响了工程文化中的风险偏好，并讨论了金融环境如何影响技术决策。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers propose a sleep-like consolidation mechanism for LLMs to stabilize learning and reduce forgetting through offline replay.</span><span class='zh'>研究者提出一种类似睡眠的巩固机制，通过离线回放帮助大语言模型稳定学习、减少遗忘，可能提升长期学习能力与训练效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers propose a sleep-like consolidation mechanism for LLMs to stabilize learning and reduce forgetting through offline replay. This could improve continual learning and efficiency for long-lived models.</span><span class='zh'>研究者提出一种类似睡眠的巩固机制，通过离线回放帮助大语言模型稳定学习、减少遗忘，可能提升长期学习能力与训练效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://akselmo.dev/posts/stop-advertising-in-your-commits/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Stop Advertising in Your Commits</span><span class='zh'>不要在提交中打广告</span></a><span class="dom">akselmo.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Advocates keeping commits clean of promotional content, stressing reproducibility and auditability.</span><span class='zh'>强调提交记录应干净，不夹带广告内容，关注可重复性与审计性。</span></p>

The post suggests practical practices like lean commit messages, separating marketing from code changes, and keeping promos out of the history.</span><span class='zh'>强调提交记录应干净，不夹带广告内容，关注可重复性与审计性。给出实践建议，如使用简洁的提交信息、将营销内容与代码改动分离、避免在历史中出现推广信息。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://journal.stuffwithstuff.com/2015/02/01/what-color-is-your-function/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://journal.stuffwithstuff.com/image/dogshot_square.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='journal.stuffwithstuff.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">50</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://journal.stuffwithstuff.com/2015/02/01/what-color-is-your-function/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What color is your function? (2015)</span><span class='zh'>你的函数是什么颜色？（2015）</span></a><span class="dom">journal.stuffwithstuff.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>What color is your function? uses a playful color metaphor to categorize functions by effects and purity, encouraging readers to think about side effects, referential transparency, and how language design shapes code reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章用有趣的颜色隐喻来划分函数的副作用与纯净性，鼓励开发者在语言设计与代码可读性之间做权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>What color is your function? uses a playful color metaphor to categorize functions by effects and purity, encouraging readers to think about side effects, referential transparency, and how language design shapes code reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章用有趣的颜色隐喻来划分函数的副作用与纯净性，鼓励开发者在语言设计与代码可读性之间做权衡。它促使读者思考语言特性如何影响程序的推理难度与可维护性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/business/spain-blocks-prediction-markets-polymarket-kalshi-over-lack-gambling-licences-2026-05-26/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spain blocks prediction markets Polymarket, Kalshi over lack of gambling licence</span><span class='zh'>西班牙因缺乏赌博许可而封锁 Polymarket 与 Kalshi 预测市场</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spain blocks Polymarket and Kalshi prediction markets for lacking gambling licenses, signaling tighter European regulation for consumer-facing betting-style platforms.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙因缺乏赌博许可，阻止 Polymarket 与 Kalshi 等预测市场运营，显示欧洲对面向消费者的预测交易平台的监管趋严。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spain blocks Polymarket and Kalshi prediction markets for lacking gambling licenses, signaling tighter European regulation for consumer-facing betting-style platforms. The move could constrain innovative fintech startups operating in Spain and set a precedent for licensing requirements.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙因缺乏赌博许可，阻止 Polymarket 与 Kalshi 等预测市场运营，显示欧洲对面向消费者的预测交易平台的监管趋严。此举可能限制在西班牙运营的创新金融科技公司，并为许可制度设立新基准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iqddnwKF8HQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zig 2026: No-AI Policy, $670K Foundation, Left GitHub and Why Zig Isn&#x27;t 1.0 [video]</span><span class='zh'>Zig 2026：禁用 AI、67 万美元基金会、退出 GitHub 与为何还未 1.0【视频】</span></a><span class="dom">youtube.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer update outlines Zig project&#x27;s 2026 choices: an explicit no-AI policy for contributions, a $670K foundation fund to support development, moving away from GitHub hosting, and reasons the language hasn&#x27;t reached 1.0 yet (stability, API decisions and ecosystem maturity).</span><span class='zh'>一段开发者更新说明了 Zig 项目 2026 年的决策：对贡献实行明确的禁用 AI 政策、成立 67 万美元的基金支持开发、脱离 GitHub 托管，以及尚未达成 1.0 的原因（稳定性、API 抉择与生态成熟度）。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer update outlines Zig project&#x27;s 2026 choices: an explicit no-AI policy for contributions, a $670K foundation fund to support development, moving away from GitHub hosting, and reasons the language hasn&#x27;t reached 1.0 yet (stability, API decisions and ecosystem maturity). The segment highlights governance, funding and independence as focal concerns for language sustainability.</span><span class='zh'>一段开发者更新说明了 Zig 项目 2026 年的决策：对贡献实行明确的禁用 AI 政策、成立 67 万美元的基金支持开发、脱离 GitHub 托管，以及尚未达成 1.0 的原因（稳定性、API 抉择与生态成熟度）。视频凸显治理、资金与独立性是语言可持续发展的核心问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nolanlawson.com/2026/05/25/using-ai-to-write-better-code-more-slowly/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using AI to write better code more slowly</span><span class='zh'>用 AI 慢下来写出更好的代码</span></a><span class="dom">nolanlawson.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI can help you write code, but slowing down can improve quality.</span><span class='zh'>AI 可以协助写代码，但放慢节奏能提升质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI can help you write code, but slowing down can improve quality. The piece argues against rushing to rely on AI; instead implement deliberate review, stronger tests, and iterative refactoring. The key takeaway: use AI as a co-pilot, not a speed boost.</span><span class='zh'>AI 可以协助写代码，但放慢节奏能提升质量。文章反对靠 AI 提速的做法，主张进行有意识的评审、加强测试和迭代重构。核心在于将 AI 视为合作者，而非提速工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tinycld.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Open-source Workspace (mail,docs,spreadsheet,drive) web/iOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：开源的工作区（邮箱、文档、表格、云盘）Web/iOS</span></a><span class="dom">tinycld.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open-source, self-hosted workspace bundle provides mail, docs, spreadsheets, and drive access on Web and iOS, offering a privacy-respecting alternative to SaaS.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个开源、可自托管的工作区，涵盖邮箱、文档、表格和云盘，提供 Web 和 iOS 端访问，作为对传统 SaaS 的隐私友好替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open-source, self-hosted workspace bundle provides mail, docs, spreadsheets, and drive access on Web and iOS, offering a privacy-respecting alternative to SaaS. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dropbox confirms leadership transition as founder and CEO Drew Houston steps down; the move signals potential strategic pivots around collaboration features, AI-enabled products, and enterprise offerings.</span><span class='zh'>Dropbox 宣布领导层变动，创始人兼首席执行官 Drew Houston 将卸任，这可能推动在协作功能、AI 化产品及企业级服务方面的战略调整。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dropbox confirms leadership transition as founder and CEO Drew Houston steps down; the move signals potential strategic pivots around collaboration features, AI-enabled products, and enterprise offerings. The incoming leadership will shape the company&#x27;s direction in a competitive cloud and productivity market.</span><span class='zh'>Dropbox 宣布领导层变动，创始人兼首席执行官 Drew Houston 将卸任，这可能推动在协作功能、AI 化产品及企业级服务方面的战略调整。新任领导团队将决定公司在竞争激烈的云存储与生产力市场中的走向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.signalbloom.ai/posts/outsourcing-plus-localai-will-soon-become-more-economical-vs-frontier-labs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Outsourcing plus local AI will soon become more economical vs. frontier labs</span><span class='zh'>外包+本地 AI 将很快比 Frontier Labs 更具成本效益</span></a><span class="dom">signalbloom.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that combining outsourcing inference with local AI runtimes will reduce costs relative to more centralized frontier-labs stacks, driven by commodity hardware and open models.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，将外包推理与本地 AI 运行时结合，将在成本上优于“Frontier Labs”一类的集中化方案，得益于通用硬件和开源模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that combining outsourcing inference with local AI runtimes will reduce costs relative to more centralized frontier-labs stacks, driven by commodity hardware and open models. It discusses the economic shift toward on-prem or edge inference and the implications for AI vendors.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，将外包推理与本地 AI 运行时结合，将在成本上优于“Frontier Labs”一类的集中化方案，得益于通用硬件和开源模型。这意味着向本地化、边缘推理的经济模型转变，将改变 AI 供应商的定价与商业模式。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://martinfowler.com/articles/vibesec-reckoning.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=martinfowler.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>martinfowler.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">65</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://martinfowler.com/articles/vibesec-reckoning.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The VibeSec Reckoning</span><span class='zh'>VibeSec 的清算：关于情感驱动的安全文化的反思</span></a><span class="dom">martinfowler.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Martin Fowler’s VibeSec Reckoning critiques security discourse that leans on vibes and culture, urging more concrete risk-based practices.</span><span class='zh'>Martin Fowler 的 VibeSec 清算批评了过于依赖情感与文化的安全话语，呼吁采用更明确的风险导向做法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Martin Fowler’s VibeSec Reckoning critiques security discourse that leans on vibes and culture, urging more concrete risk-based practices. It pushes for measurable outcomes, better engineering discipline, and transparent security metrics. The piece challenges teams to move beyond mood-driven narratives.</span><span class='zh'>Martin Fowler 的 VibeSec 清算批评了过于依赖情感与文化的安全话语，呼吁采用更明确的风险导向做法。它推动可衡量结果、更加严格的工程纪律与透明的安全指标。该文促使团队超越情绪驱动的叙事走向实质性安全实践。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>57</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48294670' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>17</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48292718" data-id="48292718" data-tags="AI|Programming|Open Source|Legal|Code" data-score="64" data-comments="8" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48292718" data-time="1779882049">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/blob/master/AI_POLICY.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">66</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/blob/master/AI_POLICY.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ripgrep AI Policy</span><span class='zh'>Ripgrep 的 AI 使用政策</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A concise policy laying out acceptable AI-related uses of the ripgrep codebase—covering whether contributors may use models to generate code, restrictions on training models with repository contents, and expectations for attribution and license compliance.</span><span class='zh'>一份简明的政策说明了对 ripgrep 代码库使用 AI 的可接受方式，包括是否允许用模型生成代码、用仓库内容训练模型的限制，以及对归属与许可合规的要求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A concise policy laying out acceptable AI-related uses of the ripgrep codebase—covering whether contributors may use models to generate code, restrictions on training models with repository contents, and expectations for attribution and license compliance. It reflects a growing trend of projects setting explicit rules to balance productivity gains from AI tools with legal and ethical responsibilities.</span><span class='zh'>一份简明的政策说明了对 ripgrep 代码库使用 AI 的可接受方式，包括是否允许用模型生成代码、用仓库内容训练模型的限制，以及对归属与许可合规的要求。该政策体现了越来越多项目在权衡 AI 工具带来的效率与法律伦理责任时制定明确规则的趋势。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>64</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48292718' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>8</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48293014" data-id="48293014" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="60" data-comments="23" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48293014" data-time="1779883698">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/WilliamSmithEdward/xlide_vscode" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>XLIDE: VBA without excel</span><span class='zh'>XLIDE：无需 Excel 即可使用 VBA</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>XLIDE lets you author and run VBA code outside Excel, offering a path to automate or port macros in alternative toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>XLIDE 允许在不依赖 Excel 的情况下编写和运行 VBA 代码，为在替代工具链中自动化或迁移宏提供了新途径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>XLIDE lets you author and run VBA code outside Excel, offering a path to automate or port macros in alternative toolchains. It opens opportunities for teams with VBA-heavy workflows to reduce Excel licensing friction. The project signals a broader trend toward decoupling legacy automation from spreadsheets.</span><span class='zh'>XLIDE 允许在不依赖 Excel 的情况下编写和运行 VBA 代码，为在替代工具链中自动化或迁移宏提供了新途径。它为大量使用 VBA 的团队减少了对 Excel 许可的依赖，体现了将遗留自动化与表格应用解耦的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Atomically precise mechanosynthesis of carbon structures on hydrogenated Silicon</span><span class='zh'>在氢化硅上进行原子级精确的碳结构机械合成</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study demonstrates atomically precise mechanosynthesis of carbon nanostructures on hydrogenated silicon, hinting at integration opportunities between carbon nanomaterials and silicon electronics.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究展示了在氢化硅表面进行原子级精确的碳结构机械合成，为碳基纳米材料与硅电子学的集成打开了新机会。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study demonstrates atomically precise mechanosynthesis of carbon nanostructures on hydrogenated silicon, hinting at integration opportunities between carbon nanomaterials and silicon electronics. This advances nanoscale fabrication concepts with potential device-level implications. The result pushes toward new materials-for-logic directions.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究展示了在氢化硅表面进行原子级精确的碳结构机械合成，为碳基纳米材料与硅电子学的集成打开了新机会。这推动了纳米级制造的概念，未来可能对器件级应用产生影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Posthorn is a self-hosted mail gateway project aimed at simplifying mail routing, spam filtering, and self-hosted email infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>Posthorn 是一个自托管邮件网关项目，目标是简化邮件路由、垃圾邮件过滤以及自托管的邮件基础设施。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Posthorn is a self-hosted mail gateway project aimed at simplifying mail routing, spam filtering, and self-hosted email infrastructure. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Minicor, a YC-backed startup, offers scalable Windows desktop automation for enterprises, enabling scripted workflows across large fleets.</span><span class='zh'>YC 资本支持的 Minicor 提供面向企业的 Windows 桌面自动化，支持在大规模设备上执行可重复的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Minicor, a YC-backed startup, offers scalable Windows desktop automation for enterprises, enabling scripted workflows across large fleets. The launch post highlights how their tooling addresses traditional RPA pain points like reliability, governance, and scale.</span><span class='zh'>YC 资本支持的 Minicor 提供面向企业的 Windows 桌面自动化，支持在大规模设备上执行可重复的工作流。其产品定位旨在解决传统机器人流程自动化在可靠性、治理和可扩展性方面的痛点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://badhost.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BadHost – CVE-2026-48710: Starlette Host-Header Auth Bypass</span><span class='zh'>BadHost – CVE-2026-48710：Starlette 主机头认证绕过</span></a><span class="dom">badhost.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A vulnerability in Starlette (CVE-2026-48710) allows an authentication bypass via crafted host headers, impacting apps relying on host header validation.</span><span class='zh'>Starlette 的 CVE-2026-48710 漏洞通过操控的主机头实现认证绕过，影响依赖主机头校验的应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A vulnerability in Starlette (CVE-2026-48710) allows an authentication bypass via crafted host headers, impacting apps relying on host header validation. The issue underscores the risk of header-based auth and the need for patching and supplemental mitigations. Upgrade and review deployment configurations to reduce exposure.</span><span class='zh'>Starlette 的 CVE-2026-48710 漏洞通过操控的主机头实现认证绕过，影响依赖主机头校验的应用。此问题凸显了基于头信息的认证风险，需要尽快打补丁并加强访问控制。请升级并检查部署配置以降低暴露度。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece demonstrates techniques for implementing an overhead camera view in the C64 BASIC era, including memory layout, scrolling, and tile mapping to render larger maps on limited hardware. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tunecat offers a lightweight, open-source internet radio solution focused on simplicity and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>Tunecat 提供一个简洁、开源的网络电台方案，强调易用性和稳定的流媒体体验，适合爱好者搭建个人广播设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tunecat offers a lightweight, open-source internet radio solution focused on simplicity and reliability. It targets easy streaming and local network compatibility, appealing to hobbyists who want a clean, offline-friendly radio appliance.</span><span class='zh'>Tunecat 提供一个简洁、开源的网络电台方案，强调易用性和稳定的流媒体体验，适合爱好者搭建个人广播设备。它面向跨设备播放和本地网络场景，追求低资源占用与简单配置。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.servethehome.com/what-is-a-direct-attach-copper-dac-cable/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What Is a Direct Attach Copper (DAC) Cable? (2021)</span><span class='zh'>直接附加铜缆（DAC）是什么</span></a><span class="dom">servethehome.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DAC cables are short-range, high-speed copper interconnects used for server connections, offering cost and power advantages over fiber for inside-rack links.</span><span class='zh'>直接附加铜缆（DAC）是一种用于服务器的短距离高速铜缆，相较光纤在机架内链路具有成本与功耗优势。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DAC cables are short-range, high-speed copper interconnects used for server connections, offering cost and power advantages over fiber for inside-rack links. They bring simplicity and low latency but trade off maximum distance. This staple of data-center design remains relevant as workloads shift toward dense, power-efficient topologies.</span><span class='zh'>直接附加铜缆（DAC）是一种用于服务器的短距离高速铜缆，相较光纤在机架内链路具有成本与功耗优势。它的优点是简单、低延迟，但距离受限。DAC 仍然是数据中心设计中的重要选择，尤其在追求高密度与低功耗的拓扑中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mmapped.blog/posts/51-interleaved-deltas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Interleaved Deltas</span><span class='zh'>交错增量编码</span></a><span class="dom">mmapped.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores a data encoding technique called interleaved deltas, which interleaves small delta differences to improve compression and data locality in memory-mapped contexts.</span><span class='zh'>本文介绍一种名为“交错增量”的数据编码技巧，通过把增量差分交错排列来提升压缩效率和内存局部性，尤其适合处理时间序列或索引数据时的带宽和缓存效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores a data encoding technique called interleaved deltas, which interleaves small delta differences to improve compression and data locality in memory-mapped contexts. The approach can boost efficiency for time-series or indexed data.</span><span class='zh'>本文介绍一种名为“交错增量”的数据编码技巧，通过把增量差分交错排列来提升压缩效率和内存局部性，尤其适合处理时间序列或索引数据时的带宽和缓存效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepswe.datacurve.ai/blog" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSWE: A contamination-free benchmark for long-horizon coding agents</span><span class='zh'>DeepSWE：用于长期代码生成任务的无污染基准</span></a><span class="dom">deepswe.datacurve.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSWE introduces a contamination-free benchmark for long-horizon coding agents, ensuring test data isn’t tainted by training data.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSWE 提供一个无污染的基准，用于评估面向长期代码生成任务的模型，确保评测数据不被训练数据污染。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSWE introduces a contamination-free benchmark for long-horizon coding agents, ensuring test data isn’t tainted by training data. This enables fairer evaluation of planning, memory, and multi-step reasoning in code-generation AI.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSWE 提供一个无污染的基准，用于评估面向长期代码生成任务的模型，确保评测数据不被训练数据污染。该基准有助于更公平地衡量代码生成AI在规划、记忆和多步推理方面的表现。</span></p>

It reflects the broader trend of integrating AI and automation into digital photography processes.</span><span class='zh'>Phloto 引入了一种简化的照片处理工作流，旨在简化编辑、元数据处理和导出等步骤。这体现了对日常创作流程中减少摩擦的用心自动化，并反映了将 AI 与自动化整合到数字摄影流程的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://anselmschueler.com/blogposts/2025-c-pointers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>C array types are weird</span><span class='zh'>C 语言中的数组类型真的很奇怪</span></a><span class="dom">anselmschueler.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>It reinforces why a solid grasp of the array-pointer boundary is essential for safe, correct C programming.</span><span class='zh'>文章揭示了 C 语言数组类型中的若干微妙怪异之处，包括数组如何衰变为指针、指针运算与下标访问的交互，以及容易踩坑的边界情况。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>It reinforces why a solid grasp of the array-pointer boundary is essential for safe, correct C programming.</span><span class='zh'>文章揭示了 C 语言数组类型中的若干微妙怪异之处，包括数组如何衰变为指针、指针运算与下标访问的交互，以及容易踩坑的边界情况。强调理解数组与指针之间的界限对实现安全、正确的 C 编程至关重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A modern Typst template for Pandoc workflows that brings high-quality typesetting, native typography and easier citation handling to document conversions.</span><span class='zh'>一个面向 Pandoc 的现代 Typst 模板，为文档转换带来更好的版面排版、原生排字和更方便的引用处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A modern Typst template for Pandoc workflows that brings high-quality typesetting, native typography and easier citation handling to document conversions. It streamlines producing print-ready PDFs from Markdown and other sources while leveraging Typst&#x27;s layout features to replace traditional LaTeX templates.</span><span class='zh'>一个面向 Pandoc 的现代 Typst 模板，为文档转换带来更好的版面排版、原生排字和更方便的引用处理。它简化了从 Markdown 等源生成印刷级 PDF 的流程，利用 Typst 的布局能力替代传统 LaTeX 模板。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04950" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Investigating how prompt politeness affects LLM accuracy (2025)</span><span class='zh'>研究提示礼貌如何影响大模型准确性（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An empirical study tests whether polite phrasing of prompts changes LLM outputs and accuracy across tasks, finding mostly small and task-dependent effects.</span><span class='zh'>一项实证研究检验了礼貌用语是否会改变大模型在不同任务上的输出与准确性，发现影响普遍较小且依赖任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An empirical study tests whether polite phrasing of prompts changes LLM outputs and accuracy across tasks, finding mostly small and task-dependent effects. The paper suggests politeness can alter model calibration or response framing but doesn&#x27;t consistently boost objective correctness, so prompt tone matters more for user experience than raw accuracy in many cases.</span><span class='zh'>一项实证研究检验了礼貌用语是否会改变大模型在不同任务上的输出与准确性，发现影响普遍较小且依赖任务。研究表明礼貌可能影响模型的置信度或回应风格，但并不稳定地提高客观正确率，因此提示语气在用户体验上更有影响，而非始终提升准确性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A deep dive into an 8-bit-era one-liner that generates a maze, extracting lessons about clever encoding, minimal state machines and the power of constraints in programming. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thonking.ai/p/strangely-matrix-multiplications" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Matrix Multiplications on GPUs Run Faster When Given “Predictable” Data (2024)</span><span class='zh'>GPU 的矩阵乘法在数据可预测时跑得更快（2024）</span></a><span class="dom">thonking.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New findings show GPUs speed up matrix multiplication when input data patterns are predictable, reducing memory latency.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，当输入数据模式可预测时，GPU 的矩阵乘法速度更快，降低了内存延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New findings show GPUs speed up matrix multiplication when input data patterns are predictable, reducing memory latency. This has practical implications for ML workloads, benchmarking, and compiler optimizations. It suggests data layout and pre-processing can meaningfully influence performance.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，当输入数据模式可预测时，GPU 的矩阵乘法速度更快，降低了内存延迟。这对机器学习负载、基准测试和编译器优化有实际影响，暗示数据布局和预处理可以显著影响性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.superjumpmagazine.com/the-forgotten-art-of-the-lan-party/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Forgotten Art of the LAN Party (2023)</span><span class='zh'>被遗忘的局域网聚会艺术（2023）</span></a><span class="dom">superjumpmagazine.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Revisits LAN parties as a social-technical phenomenon: in 2023 they remain valuable for hands-on hardware tinkering, local multiplayer energy, and community building that online-only ecosystems can&#x27;t replicate.</span><span class='zh'>本文回顾 2023 年的局域网聚会文化，认为线下的设备共享、现场改装和本地多人互动仍然带来独特的技术乐趣与社交氛围，线上生态难以完全替代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Revisits LAN parties as a social-technical phenomenon: in 2023 they remain valuable for hands-on hardware tinkering, local multiplayer energy, and community building that online-only ecosystems can&#x27;t replicate. The piece also offers practical tips to revive the scene, from retro rigs to local networks and shared screens.</span><span class='zh'>本文回顾 2023 年的局域网聚会文化，认为线下的设备共享、现场改装和本地多人互动仍然带来独特的技术乐趣与社交氛围，线上生态难以完全替代。文章还给出重振这类聚会的思路，如组织经济友好的装置、优化局域网和共享显示等。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://borretti.me/article/human-bottlenecks" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Human Bottlenecks</span><span class='zh'>人类瓶颈</span></a><span class="dom">borretti.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Human Bottlenecks argues that human limitations shape system design, proposing interfaces and processes that reduce cognitive load, delay fatigue, and improve reliability in technology workflows.</span><span class='zh'>《人类瓶颈》主张人类局限性决定系统设计，应提出降低认知负荷、延缓疲劳、提升技术工作流程可靠性的界面与流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Human Bottlenecks argues that human limitations shape system design, proposing interfaces and processes that reduce cognitive load, delay fatigue, and improve reliability in technology workflows.</span><span class='zh'>《人类瓶颈》主张人类局限性决定系统设计，应提出降低认知负荷、延缓疲劳、提升技术工作流程可靠性的界面与流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.rockpapershotgun.com/splinter-cell-veteran-says-realistic-modern-lighting-has-screwed-up-stealth-games-it-gets-very-hard-to-tell-whats-light-whats-shadow-whats-dark-whats-safe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Splinter Cell veteran says realistic modern lighting has screwed up stealth game</span><span class='zh'>《潜龙谍影》资深玩家：现实照明破坏潜行游戏</span></a><span class="dom">rockpapershotgun.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Splinter Cell veteran argues that modern lighting realism makes it nearly impossible to distinguish light, shadow, and safe paths, breaking classic stealth cues.</span><span class='zh'>这名《潜龙谍影》资深玩家认为当代光照的真实感让玩家难以分辨光线、阴影与安全区，削弱了潜行设计的清晰度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Splinter Cell veteran argues that modern lighting realism makes it nearly impossible to distinguish light, shadow, and safe paths, breaking classic stealth cues. This critique highlights the tension between photoreal visuals and clear gameplay signals, suggesting designers may need stronger visual rules or scripted stealth to preserve tension.</span><span class='zh'>这名《潜龙谍影》资深玩家认为当代光照的真实感让玩家难以分辨光线、阴影与安全区，削弱了潜行设计的清晰度。此观点凸显了写实光照与游戏性之间的矛盾，可能促使开发者在视觉语言上做出更明确的规则或采用更强的剧情化潜行判定来维持紧张感。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/logannye/rosalind" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rosalind: A genomics toolkit in Rust running whole-genome pipelines on a laptop</span><span class='zh'>Rosalind：用 Rust 编写的基因组学工具包，可在笔记本电脑上运行全基因组管线</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rosalind is a genomics toolkit written in Rust that runs whole-genome pipelines on a laptop.</span><span class='zh'>Rosalind 是用 Rust 编写的基因组学工具包，可以在笔记本电脑上运行完整的全基因组管线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rosalind is a genomics toolkit written in Rust that runs whole-genome pipelines on a laptop. It demonstrates how Rust&#x27;s performance and safety can empower researchers to carry out end-to-end analyses locally, reducing reliance on cloud or clusters. This portability could lower barriers for small labs and field work.</span><span class='zh'>Rosalind 是用 Rust 编写的基因组学工具包，可以在笔记本电脑上运行完整的全基因组管线。其展示了 Rust 的高性能与内存安全如何让研究者在本地完成一整套分析，减少对云端或计算集群的依赖。这种便携性有望降低小型实验室的门槛并支持现场工作。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A dive into opaque types for Python typing, explaining how you can hide internal representations behind a stable API to improve abstraction, maintainability, and type safety.</span><span class='zh'>本文介绍 Python 类型系统中的不透明类型，讲解如何将内部实现隐藏在稳定的 API 之后，以提升抽象性、可维护性和静态类型的安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A dive into opaque types for Python typing, explaining how you can hide internal representations behind a stable API to improve abstraction, maintainability, and type safety.</span><span class='zh'>本文介绍 Python 类型系统中的不透明类型，讲解如何将内部实现隐藏在稳定的 API 之后，以提升抽象性、可维护性和静态类型的安全性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A historical video about IBM System/360 file organization, covering record formats, addressing, and data layout decisions that influenced early mainframe software design.</span><span class='zh'>这段关于 System/360 的文件组织的历史视频，介绍了记录格式、寻址方式和当时对数据组织的设计决策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A historical video about IBM System/360 file organization, covering record formats, addressing, and data layout decisions that influenced early mainframe software design.</span><span class='zh'>这段关于 System/360 的文件组织的历史视频，介绍了记录格式、寻址方式和当时对数据组织的设计决策。内容帮助理解早期主机软件设计的原则，并影响后续体系结构的演进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neugierig.org/software/blog/2026/05/theseus-wasm.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Theseus: Translating Win32 to WASM</span><span class='zh'>Theseus：将 Win32 转译为 WASM</span></a><span class="dom">neugierig.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Theseus aims to translate Win32 binaries into WebAssembly, enabling legacy Windows applications to run in the browser or on non-Windows runtimes.</span><span class='zh'>Theseus 旨在将 Win32 二进制转换为 WebAssembly，使传统 Windows 应用能够在浏览器或非 Windows 的运行时中运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Theseus aims to translate Win32 binaries into WebAssembly, enabling legacy Windows applications to run in the browser or on non-Windows runtimes. The approach showcases WASM as a portability layer with potential for reducing platform lock-in, though real-world viability remains to be seen. It highlights ongoing efforts to bridge desktop software to the web.</span><span class='zh'>Theseus 旨在将 Win32 二进制转换为 WebAssembly，使传统 Windows 应用能够在浏览器或非 Windows 的运行时中运行。该思路展示了 WASM 作为跨平台层的潜力，可能降低对特定平台的依赖，但实际落地的可行性仍待观察，反映出将桌面软件桥接到网页的持续努力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Examines how archivists decide what to keep, using Kerouac as a touchstone to discuss the tension between spontaneity and preservation.</span><span class='zh'>讨论档案学在选择保留什么时的权衡，以凯鲁亚克为例探讨自发性与保存之间的张力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Examines how archivists decide what to keep, using Kerouac as a touchstone to discuss the tension between spontaneity and preservation. The piece considers how digital archiving will shape what about authors and their work survives for future readers.</span><span class='zh'>讨论档案学在选择保留什么时的权衡，以凯鲁亚克为例探讨自发性与保存之间的张力。文章还思考数字化存档将如何影响未来读者对作者及其作品的留存与理解。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rajveerbachkaniwala.com/blog/2026/05/24/on-the-difficulty-of-pasting-a-picture/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Ctrl+V won&#x27;t paste images in Claude Code on WSL, with a fix</span><span class='zh'>为什么在 WSL 的 Claude Code 中 Ctrl+V 贴不上图片，以及修复方法</span></a><span class="dom">rajveerbachkaniwala.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical debugging guide explaining why pasting images into a Windows Subsystem for Linux app fails—clipboard format and X/Wayland interoperability issues—and provides a working workaround (clipboard bridge or helper script) to transfer image data reliably.</span><span class='zh'>一篇实用指南解释了在 WSL 中将图片粘贴到应用失败的原因：剪贴板格式和 X/Wayland 互通问题，并给出了可行的修复（剪贴板桥接或辅助脚本）以可靠传输图像数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical debugging guide explaining why pasting images into a Windows Subsystem for Linux app fails—clipboard format and X/Wayland interoperability issues—and provides a working workaround (clipboard bridge or helper script) to transfer image data reliably. Useful for developers who run GUI Linux apps on WSL and need seamless clipboard support for screenshots and images.</span><span class='zh'>一篇实用指南解释了在 WSL 中将图片粘贴到应用失败的原因：剪贴板格式和 X/Wayland 互通问题，并给出了可行的修复（剪贴板桥接或辅助脚本）以可靠传输图像数据。对在 WSL 上运行 GUI Linux 应用并需要无缝剪贴板支持的开发者很有帮助。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://randsinrepose.com/archives/the-ask/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Ask</span><span class='zh'>请求的艺术</span></a><span class="dom">randsinrepose.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Advice on how to make clear, responsible requests in work and life—framing asks with context, desired outcomes and constraints so others can respond effectively.</span><span class='zh'>关于如何在工作与生活中提出清晰、有责任感的请求：用背景、期望结果和限制来框定，让对方能有效回应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Advice on how to make clear, responsible requests in work and life—framing asks with context, desired outcomes and constraints so others can respond effectively. The key takeaway is that good requests reduce friction, clarify ownership, and increase the chance of getting what you need without creating hidden obligations.</span><span class='zh'>关于如何在工作与生活中提出清晰、有责任感的请求：用背景、期望结果和限制来框定，让对方能有效回应。要点是：好的请求能减少摩擦、明确责任并提高达成目标的可能，同时避免产生隐含义务。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shares practical lessons from building online mini games with Elixir on the back end and Swift on the client, covering concurrency, real-time features, and networking concerns.</span><span class='zh'>作者分享用 Elixir 构建在线小游戏后学到的经验，涵盖并发、实时特性与客户端-服务器架构的取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shares practical lessons from building online mini games with Elixir on the back end and Swift on the client, covering concurrency, real-time features, and networking concerns. The author emphasizes architecture choices that balance responsiveness with maintainability.</span><span class='zh'>作者分享用 Elixir 构建在线小游戏后学到的经验，涵盖并发、实时特性与客户端-服务器架构的取舍。文章强调在可维护性与响应性之间取得平衡的重要性，以及在分层设计中的实际教训。</span></p>
